Pinned: `getPosition` (25 emissions), `getMActor` (5), `getModel`/`getFrameCtrl` (4), `getConsole`, `TPathNode::getPoint` (raw read in the header is a wash: +2/-2).
- **When an UNUSED function's only expansion site is another UNUSED function, the two sizes move together at a fixed offset**, pinning both bodies where either size admits many: `TDrawSyncManager::end()` = `if (smInstance) { delete smInstance; smInstance = nullptr; }` (0x80) and the dtor = `OSSendMessage(&mMessageQueue, (void*)0x10000, 1); OSJoinThread(&mProcessingThread, nullptr);` (0x88), both exact; the loop in `threadFunc` exits only for a message in [0x10000, 0x80000000).

## Structural pass IV (2026-09-18): the inline-level ladder and four real bugs

- **A missing weak callee is a depth measurement, not a per-site inlining mystery.** When the map lists a small weak accessor as a real symbol of a TU and our object reports it MISSING, the enclosing expansions are missing, and thin TU-local `static inline` forwarders measure exactly how many. Each forwarder is worth one level; a *statement-bearing* helper is not a substitute (its statements spend the budget instead, so it buys one level at most). `killer`'s chase nerve needed four levels: 1 -> 95.0 (JMASSin inlined), 2 -> 96.4 (`bl JMASin`), 3 -> 98.2 (`bl set<f32>`), 4 -> 98.9 (`bl MsSin`, `bl MsCos`, `bl set<f32>` -- retail's call set) and the third callee-saved FPR appears; `MsCos`, `MsSin` and `TVec3::set<f>` went 0% -> 100%. `MapObjRicco::fireObj` needed exactly three (two leave `getMActor` expanded, four also push `J3DFrameCtrl::setFrame` out of line, which retail inlines); `getMActor` 0% -> 100%, 98.95 -> 99.92. Refuted on `TPinnaCoaster::control` (one level changes nothing and loses 8 bytes of frame).
- **A matrix translation read is `m[0][3]`, `m[1][3]`, `m[2][3]`.** Two independent units had it as row 3: `TMapObjGeneral::calcRootMatrix` (97.5 -> 100 together with calling `getTakingMtx()` on `mHolder`, not `this`, and dropping a dead `TVec3 pos` local that reserved 32 bytes) and `TTelesa::initAttacker` (99.66 -> 99.68). `grep -n '\[3\]\[0\]' src/` finds the rest; the sites left are all in units whose functions already match or are UNUSED with size-insensitive bodies.
- **A constructor's component order is visible in the store order, not just the values**: `TMareEventBumpyWall::bumpUpZ`/`bumpDownZ` build `TVec3(0, 0, z)`, not `TVec3(z, 0, 0)` (the zero literal loads *before* the joint read), 94.9/96.1 -> 97.4/98.6.
- **A vector written back component-wise is `lfs/stfs` per field; retail's `lwz/stw` pairs are a whole-object assignment.** `TKukkuBall::bind` ends in `mPosition = pos;` (94.2 -> 96.3, and the UNUSED body is size-exact at 0x128). The inverse tell also holds: `TSelectShineManager::initData`'s two chained `lfs/stfs` TVec2 copies are *not* `operator=`.
- **A class's tail members come from the `new` size, not from the last member we can name**: `TMovieDirector` is 0x3c (`li r3, 0x3c` in Application), so a pointer lives at 0x38 and the ctor zeroes 0x34 and 0x38 (95.65 -> 100).
- **Reading a member per use through its accessor un-CSEs it, and that is a statement-shape choice, not a lever**: `TMapObjBase::calcReflectingVelocity` is `wall->getNormal().x` at each component (retail re-reads `0x34(r4)` although the value is live) plus `onePlus * (dot * n.c)` grouping and `dot` declared *before* `onePlus` -- all three together, 71.4 -> 100. Declaration order between two scalars decided the schedule, not just the registers.
- Recorded: `MsMtxSetXYZRPH`'s destination wants the matrix address bound to a named `MtxPtr` when the matrix is used twice (`TTelesa::perform`, with the y argument being `checkGround`'s return and `Mtx` declared *before* the ground-plane pointer, 88.8 -> 100). Refuted and left as TODOs: the four `bumpUp/Down` functions' shared 4-byte low region (the two-argument `startSoundActor` is +8, `&getPosition()` +0), MWCC reassociating `(a - 1) + b` in `TMareEventDepressWall::initCommon` (spelling the subtraction first at least fixes the load order and the add's operands, 26 markers -> 9), `TMario::checkDescent`'s signed `cmpwi` on a byte flag (`!= TRUE` and `u8` both still give `cmplwi`), `TMenuDirector::rsetup`'s unfolded `i == 17 || i == 18` (an inlined predicate makes it worse, 99.4 -> 99.0), and `TMapWire::getPointPosOnLine` as `out->set(...)` (lands `move()`'s out-of-line `bl set<f>` but costs `getPointPosOnWire` 95.8 -> 48.9).

## Structural pass 167 (2026-09-18): four statement shapes and three real bugs

- **A one-case `switch` is the only spelling that emits a *positive* test plus an explicit jump past the body** (`beq <case>; b <default>`); every `if` form, including `if (c) return;` and an empty then with an `else`, folds to a single inverted branch. Two instances, and both had the condition inverted in our source: `TMarDirector::movement` is `switch (mState) { case STATE_UNK4: movement_game(); break; }` (91.25 -> 100) and `movement_game` wraps its whole body in `switch (unk124) { case 0: ... break; }` (97.42 -> 99.86, 48 bytes of frame left).
- **`if (T* p = f())` (a declaration in the condition) emits `mr.` -- move and test in one instruction -- where a preceding named local plus `if (p)` gives `cmplwi` + `addi`.** Both spellings are otherwise identical; this is the tell for retail's fused pointer test (`movement_game`'s `findNearestTakeNPC` result).
- **A reference-returning accessor read *twice* through a named reference materialises the address** (`add rX, base, idx` then `lfs 0(rX)` / `stfs 0(rX)`), while `accessor(...) -= v` gives `lfsx`/`stfsx` and two accessor expansions give the right addressing at +4 of frame per extra expansion. `TMapObjPlane::depress`'s four grid updates are `f32& h = heightAt(x, z); h = h - rate * (...);` (94.74 -> 100, frame exact). Refuted: `f32&` with `-=` (lfsx, +0x10 frame), a `f32*` accessor, pointer arithmetic inside `heightAt` (81.8%), raw `mHeightMap[...]` twice (asm exact, frame 0x10 short).
- **MWCC merges a ground-type compare chain down to its first group when the guarded statement is gone, whatever the chain's spelling, but keeps the whole chain when it comes from an inlined predicate.** `TNerveMameGessoGraphJumpWander`'s reached-goal arm holds a dead water test (`if (isReachedToGoal()) { if (isAirborne()) { if (<6-type water chain>) {} } } else { walkBehavior }`) covering six types -- `isWaterSurface()` minus `BG_TYPE_SHADED_POOL` -- 97.65 -> 98.89 with four instructions left. Refuted: the chain at the site (drops the range), the negated `&&` form (three unfolded terms), an empty `else` (inert).
- Real bugs: `TMario::electricDamage` ends in `MARIO_STATUS_ROCKET_LANDING` (0x88d), not `MARIO_STATUS_WAIT` (98.35 -> 100); `TPollutionLayer::initLayerInfo`'s 128/128 threshold arm is `FIRE || ELECTRIC`, and adding the second `getPollutionType()` expansion also buys the 8 bytes of frame it was short (96.61 -> 100); `movement_game` clears hit flag 0x4, not 0x2.
- Recorded: `TRocket::calcRootMatrix` needs the model pointer bound before `getBaseTRMtx()` expands (`addi r4, r3, 0; addi r4, r4, 0x20`) and 4 bytes lower temps, but a TU-local `static inline` binding gives +8 and no second `addi`; `TEnemyManager::performShared` copies an already-materialised zero into the first loop index (`addi r3, r5, 0`) and is 4 bytes short in the `TTimeRec` colour temp; `TPollutionLayer::stampModel` wants `mMinX` loaded before `mtx[0][3]` and refuses the raw-read spelling; `CPolarSubCamera::execWallCheck_` reloads the plane normal where retail CSEs it; `TJumpBase::control` needs two un-CSE'd `sraw` shifts for its sin/cos pair.

## Structural pass 166 (2026-09-18): the relocation-target bug class

- **A relocation pointing at the wrong symbol renders with identical opcode and operands, so objdiff scores it 100% and no regression report flags it.** Detectors in the batch-166 scratchpad (`census_virt.py`: `blrl`-count asymmetry = a devirtualised call; `census_calls.py`: per-function call-set diffs; `census_reloc.py`: reloc-target name mismatches per instruction index) found 8 devirtualisations, 213 call-set diffs and 115 reloc mismatches across the 225 unlinked game units. Fixed to exact: five `TLauncher`/`TCommonLauncher` functions (`getSaveParam2()` called `TSpineEnemy::getSaveParam()` *qualified*, killing the vtable dispatch: an explicitly qualified `Base::virtual()` in a header accessor silently devirtualises), `TPakkun::perform` (`calc()` -> `calcAnm()`), `TFireWanwan::attackToMario` (`Recover` -> `RecoverGraph`), `TMario::thinkSituation` (`startVoice`, not `startSoundActor`), `emitTry` (RT/SRT callbacks swapped; 0x10 = `BIND_TO_RT_MTX`), `TCardLoad::changeScene` (`drawMessageBM`). Also `TMapObjBall::kicked` (retail spells `SMS_GetMarioHitActor()->receiveMessage()`), `TObjHitCheck::checkActorsHit` (4x `checkAndEntryGroup`, `unk10[5]`, Mario's box is damage- not attack-radius), THinokuri2 (Freeze/Damage and Stamp/Pollute swapped), BGK nerves (`setGateKeeperBGMPlayFlag`/`getGateKeeperBGMStopFlag` missing). ~100 flagged sites remain (GCConsole2, bossgesso, FlagManager, SelectMenu, MapObjMamma, sin/cos table swaps).
- `TCardSector::setCheckSum` is `mCheckSum = CalcCheckSum(this, 0x1FFC)` (size 0x50 exact) but four other sites regress: a per-site split, recorded. Two callee-saved FPRs holding render width/height mean named `f32` locals. `MapCollisionEntry::setMtx` out of class without `inline` emits global and still inlines: a fourth, non-template "weak plus `bl` at depth 1" instance. Census of small opcode residues: 648 functions (`census_struct.py`); the top 20 were already-known-open classes.

## Closure batch 168 (2026-09-18): sunmodel three closures

- **A `(func,weak)` body retail `bl`s cannot be a statement of its caller; each such pair needs its own enclosing expansion, and nested pairs one level each.** sunmodel's `calcDispRatioAndScreenPos_` is weak and called, so the `CUE_MOVE` block became a fabricated `moveSun_` expansion; one level further down made `CLBScreenFPosToSPos` a `bl` and its weak 0x114 body now exists (ctor 64 -> 100, calcDispRatio 14 -> 100, CLBScreenFPosToSPos 0 -> 98). Depth grows inward: argument-side forwarder chains never deepen the outer call, and a three-deep `static inline` chain breaks (the outermost stops inlining and emits a symbol).
- **MWCC unrolls a known-trip-count loop 8x when pointer increments sit in the body; in the `for`-increment clause it keeps a single body** (51 instructions in sunmodel's ctor). Among function-scope named locals callee-saved GPRs go r31 downward in reverse declaration order: read retail's r31/r30/r29 off a loop and reorder the declarations (two closures).
- Recorded: MarNameRefGen_BossEnemy's remaining temp is allocated last by retail (0x1c) and the level belongs inside shared `SleepBossHanachan.hpp`; MSModBgm `modBgm`'s redundant `li r31, 0` proves the store was scheduled after the pointer assignment (scheduling, not spelling), `getTiming`'s `unk0` is a named local; PacketUtil's permutation is one extra live value = the missing 8-byte object.

## Header round 30 (2026-09-18)

- **An implicit derived-to-base-typed argument is the reachable form of the depth-4 `set(const Vec&)`**: a `Vec`-typed accessor (`TCamera::getUnk124Vec()` returning `const Vec&`, added as a *sibling* of the `TVec3` one) passed to a `const TVec3<f32>&` parameter takes the converting ctor `TVec3(const Vec&)`, adding the level inside the expression that argument-side forwarder chains cannot add. Retyping the existing accessor costs `TMap::update` and `forceRequest` (a lazily-read subtraction operand wants the derived type): accessor return type is a per-site split like `operator*`. `TSunModel::perform` 95.1 -> 97.5 with the `bl set(const Vec&)` neighbourhood exact; a duplicate `squared()` expansion (8 bytes of pool, still CSE'd by MWCC) and an unnamed `2.0f - distSq` were the levers removed.
- SleepBossHanachan: moving the TU-local level into the header is byte-inert (pool order does not depend on which header supplies a level); retail calls `set<f>` with `r3 = &mShinePosition`, so the remaining shape must change the number of temps. `CLBScreenFPosToSPos` has no inlined callee at all; its 8 dead bytes fit one dead 8-byte non-trivial class local of an inlined callee (`TVec2<f32>`), no candidate helper in the map.
## Structural pass 173 (2026-09-18): four statement shapes and five real bugs

- **The redundant `bcc next; b epilogue` pair is by far the most productive tell in the census**, and every instance in this pass was a pair of separate `if (c) return;` statements that retail spells as one `if (a || b) return;`: `TMapObjBase::perform` (`checkLiveFlag(LIVE_FLAG_DEAD) || isActorType(0x4000003B)`, 99.54 -> 99.96), `setGroundCollision` (`!mMapCollisionManager || ...->mKind != KIND_MOVE`, 98.76 -> 99.90) and `startAnim` (`!anim || anim->unk0 <= param_1`, 98.47 -> 99.91 -- note the operand order, `cmplw anim->unk0, param_1` with `bgt`, so the term is `<=` on the member, not `>=` on the argument).
- **A vtable slot past the end of the declared base's vtable means the receiver is a derived class, not a wrong slot index**: `TMapObjBase::perform`'s `getModel()->viewCalc()` is J3DModel slot 0x14, but retail calls 0x1c, which only exists in `SDLModel` (`viewCalcSimple`). The model MActor builds is always an SDLModel; `TLiveActor::getModel()` most likely returns `SDLModel*` in retail (shared-header change, parked behind a cast).
- **A callee-saved FPR holding a literal that is reloaded anonymously at its other uses means one named `f32` local whose only use is that argument**: `TNerveHauntLegHaunt`'s `f32 jumpHeight = 10.0f;` before `calcVelocityToJumpToY(pos, jumpHeight, getGravityY())` loads early into f31 and costs the `fmr f1, f31` (95.87 -> 98.79 and the frame becomes exact), while the later `mPosition.y += 10.0f` stays an anonymous `lfs`.
- **An `int` local feeding an `int` parameter gives `mr`, a `u8` one `addi rD, rS, 0`**, even when the value is already zero-extended in the register: `TMapObjBase::startAnim`'s `int type = d2->unk8;`.
- **A stack address used at two call sites is bound once into a callee-saved register**; computing it per site is one instruction short each time. `TMirrorCamera::perform`'s `Mtx44Ptr projMtx = graphics->mProjMtx.mMtx;` shared by `C_MTXPerspective` and `GXSetProjection` is 91.04 -> 99.07. The member-reference form is the same lever: `TLimitKoopaJr::calcTargetDirection`'s `const TVec3<f32>& bathtubPos = bathtub->mPosition;` gives `addi r5, r3, 0x10` and takes both LimitKoopaJr nerves to 99.8.
- **A declaration whose initialiser is a constant is emitted where it is declared, so its position relative to a base-class call is readable off the prologue**: `TGCLogoDir::direct`'s `li r31, 1` precedes `bl TDirector::direct()`, i.e. `int desiredAppState = APP_STATE_DEFAULT;` comes *before* the base call (96.87 -> 99.79).
- Real bugs: `TCardManager`'s US `titles`/`comments` hold **five** entries (English, German, French, Spanish, Italian; `-str reuse` folds the five identical titles into one `@1632`) and the index is a bare `getFlag(0xA0001)` with no `!= 0x100` guard and no `+1` (getOptionWriteStream 86.95 -> 100, filledInitData_ +4.5, data 2.56 -> 56.41); `readOptionBlock_` tests `STATE_EMPTY`, not `STATE_UNREAD`; `TPoiHana::isHitValid` sets `LIVE_FLAG_HIDDEN` before returning true on message 11 (94.12 -> 100) and `isCollidMove` scales the **local copy** of `mLinearVelocity`, leaving the member alone (97.88 -> 99.85); `TMapObjBase::makeMActors` passes `anim->unk4[i].unkC` as `initMActor`'s second name, not `nullptr` (97.47 -> 98.72).
- Recorded: `initMActor`'s `param_2` is copied to a callee-saved register and never read, and both `makeMActors` expansions load `unkC` for it, so the ROM has a use whose values MWCC stripped (the walkerEnemy `getSaveParam()` pattern) -- unidentified. `TLimitKoopaJr::updateTimers` is 0x34 = six instructions per timer because every countdown materialises the timer's address; a TU-local `static inline decreaseTimer(int*)` does not reproduce it (MWCC folds `&member` back). `TCardManager::decideUseSector` keeps its result in r0 and joins with one `mr r3, r0`; the if/else, a ternary and a top-of-function declaration all put it straight in r3. `TMapObjSwitch::control` needs `mStateTimer` re-read for `playTimer`; `getStateTimer()` and a `const` predicate are both inert. `TConductor::isBossDefeated`'s pivot sends *everything but 3* to the first arm, i.e. `default:` labels it, but `case 2: default:` folds to a single compare and costs five instructions.

## Structural pass 167 (2026-09-18): the relocation-target detectors as a repo tool

- The three batch-166 detectors are now `tools/check-relocs.py` (`--mode virt|calls|reloc|all`, `-u`, `--category`, `--tsv`), ranked by unit bytes-left. Over the 248 unlinked units (game **and** library) it starts at 328 sites: virt 7, calls 207, reloc 114.
- **`reloc` mode's false positive is the compensated swap pair**: two symbols exchanged between two positions, with the *uses* exchanged to match, is a load-order or register-allocation difference and not a bug. Confirmed benign on `TConeBeam::calcVertices` (sin/cos bases in r4/r6), `TMarDirector::setupObjects` (render width/height into f30/f31), `TKoopaJrSubmarine::init` (the two joint tables). A *single* unpaired mismatch, or a pair whose uses are **not** exchanged, is real: `TTelesa::setFirstAttackPoint` really put the cosine on x (the UNUSED body stays size-exact at the map's 0x118 either way, and objdiff's score even drops 0.16 -- the class is invisible to scoring by construction).
- **`calls` mode's strongest signal is a target-only callee that appears nowhere in the function**, which is missing behaviour rather than an inlining difference. Closed this way: `MSBgm::startBGM(MSD_BGM_SHINE_APPEAR)` in `TShine::appearSimple` (93.2 -> 99.9), `TShine::movingUp`/`movingDown` each spinning the shine by 7 degrees and calling the discarded `MsWrap` (control 95.0 -> 99.9, and Item's `.sdata2` 5.87 -> 87.99 because the 7.0/360.0 literals were missing), `TParams::load(mPrmPath)` in both TobiPuku params ctors (two `load`s to exact), a second unconditional `MActor::setFrameRate(0.0f, 0)` in `TNerveSmallEnemyChange` (93.3 -> 99.9), `MSound::startSoundSystemSE(MSD_SE_SY_CLEAR_SIGN_BIG)` in `TMapEventSinkBianco::startControl` (89.1 -> 99.9), and the `MSRandPlay` branch on `TMapStaticObj::mRandPlayHandle` (92.1 -> 99.3; the `gateCheck` must be spelled out, since `MSound::startSeRandPlay`'s wrapper temp is +8 of frame the ROM does not have).
- **An `O:` callee that the target never calls is a semantic bug, not a codegen one**: `TSpineEnemy::doShortCut` *popped* the next path node where retail only peeks it (`mData[mSize - 1]`, no empty guard, no decrement), and consumes it further down only once the wall probe is clear. `TSolidStack::top()` has no map symbol anywhere, so it must stay expandable: returning `T` by value makes MWCC emit an out-of-line copy of the template member, `const T&` expands (94.7 -> 99.6, and `TKumokun::moveObject` 95.3 -> 98.0 for free). Retail writes `TPathNode node; node = stack.top();` -- declared then assigned, so the default ctor's four stores precede the copy.
- **`virt` mode also fires in reverse**: retail having *more* `blrl` can mean it expanded a base body that carries virtual calls (`TGorogoro::behaveToWater` inlines all of `TRollEnemy::behaveToWater`), not that we devirtualised anything. Check which side owns the extra direct `bl` before reading it as a qualified-base call.
- `TLiveActor::unk90` is the actor's **`TSpcInterp*`**: `control()` guards on its `mStepsToDo` (0x4, signed -- the ctor takes `int steps` and it feeds `s32 mStepsLeft`) and calls `update()` through the vptr at 0x5c slot 0x10. While a script has steps queued the interpreter drives the actor, except that a spine with a current nerve or a non-empty vertebra stack still updates itself; that predicate is an inline `||` compare, not a bool helper (73.96 -> 100).
- `TFlagManager`'s option flags exist twice -- 0x7xxxx/0x8xxxx on the memory card, 0x9xxxx/0xAxxxx live -- so `correctOptFlag()` and `saveOption()` are exact mirrors (0x70000<->0x90000, 0x80000<->0xA0001, 0x70002<->0x90001). Three functions to exact.
- Parked with the evidence in the source: `MapCollisionEntry::setMtx` (still the "weak plus `bl` at depth 1" instance, hits `TMapStaticObj::init` and `TMapObjBase::makeObjAppeared`), `TWireTrap::getWireDir` refused at depth three in three nerves (a `const TVec3&` binding and `momentum.set(...)` both measured worse), `TMapEventSinkBianco::loadAfter`'s depth-two refusal of `TMapEventSinkInPollution::loadAfter`, and `TOptionControl`'s per-site `SMSGetMSound`/`stopSound` expansions.

## Reloc-target pass III (2026-09-18): big units

- **Retail's `bl`-vs-inline choice is a statement count against the 14-statement depth-1 budget, and codegen-free statements reach it**: split `length()` into `squared()` + `TUtil<f32>::sqrt()`, name an intermediate pointer, declare-then-assign a class local (`TVec3 tipPos; tipPos = ...`), `BOOL clean = TRUE; if (clean == TRUE)`. MarioSpecial's wire nerves 58-69 -> 94-97 (`wireMove`'s 15th statement keeps its `bl`); bossgesso `perform` 82 -> 98 (four such statements keep `lenFromToeToMario` a `bl`), `moveObject` 85 -> 98 (`doAttackShoot` one statement more, `doAttackGuard` two fewer: sibling functions can need opposite corrections); GCConsole2 `startDisappearTank` 70 -> 99 and `startCameraDemo` 61 -> 97 (the missing `if (onYoshi())` hide tail is also the 15-statement floor for three `bl`s).
- **Statement shapes read off the asm**: a `lbz` followed by a signed `cmpwi` means the local is `int`, not `u8`; one `cmpwi` per case value is an if/else-if chain in source order with duplicated arm bodies as separate arms (GCConsole2 nozzle dispatch 0,1,4,5,2); `insert(tex, 0, 1.0f); remove(1)` pairs are `changeTexture(tex->getTexInfo(), 0)` (SelectMenu); rumble on `&mPosition` not the trigger's; `(*gpMarioGroundPlane)->getActor()` rather than the out-of-line accessor; `startStateTimer(mDemoLightTime + 120)`; the cosine on `diff.x`, sine on `diff.z`, cos declared first (`jmaCosTable`/`jmaSinTable` land in f31/f29).
- Negative results recorded: pakkun's factory ctor flips at 5 statements (old note wrong); `SetupThreadFuncLogo` needs 4 codegen-free statements (the `while(true)/break` shape reaches 100 but costs its byte-exact callee). Remaining flagged reloc rows (GCConsole2 7, bossgesso 4, SelectMenu 2, ModelGate 2, lensflare 1) are pinned-helper inline depth; tree-wide `--mode reloc` is down to 38 rows.

## Reloc-target pass IV (2026-09-18)

- **A global read reached through its inline accessor is a one-level evaluation-order knob at a call site.** Passing `&SMS_GetMarioPos()` instead of `gpMarioPos` moves the argument's `lwz` *ahead* of the receiver's, restoring retail's right-to-left-then-`this` order (`TNerveBEelTearsMarioRecover::execute`, `TChangeStageMerrygoround::calc`, both to 100). The same substitution on the **receiver** (`SMSGetMarDirector()->fireStartDemoCamera(...)`) moves the receiver load *before* all the argument setup (`TLeanMirror::controlGoTarget` 93.5 -> 99.8). Zero frame cost in all three: it is the cheapest thing to try on any `reloc` row that is a two-symbol rotation around one `bl`.
- **`a * b * c` association is readable off the first `fmuls`**: retail's `fmuls f0, sin, unk2C` then `fmuls f0, power, f0` pins the source as `sin * unk2C * power` (`TMapObjGeneral::thrown`, +2.0).
- **Ternary arm order dates the condition's spelling**: MWCC emits the true arm out of line after a `beq`, so a retail `bne <true-arm>` with the *false* value in the fallthrough means the source negated the test (`TMarDirector::fireGetStar`: `!shine->unk190 ? Outside : Inside`).
- Real bug: `TShine::appearWithTime` was missing `MSBgm::startBGM(MSD_BGM_SHINE_APPEAR)` after the SE (83.9 -> 86.2); `appearSimple` had it.
- Refuted: named `TOrthoProj` width/height locals in `MarDirectorSetupObjects` fix the order but cost frame (98.1 -> 97.5). Flagged pairs in `CLBCalcNearNinePos`, `BathtubKiller::isAboided`, igaiga, hx_wiper, `koopajr::init`, `MapObjMamma::controlShake` are instruction-index artifacts of an adjacent residue, not wrong symbols.
- **Research seed: small global helpers retail refuses to inline.** `TFireHamuKuri::changeTevColor` compiles byte-exact at its map size 0x1cc yet we inline it into `moveObject` where retail `bl`s it; same shape in `THamuKuriManager::requestSerialKill` (via `TFireHamuKuri::isHitValid`), `TBossGesso::showMessage`, `TBossPakkun::showMessage`, `TBossTelesa::forceAllItemKill`, and the file-static `SMS_getShineID` (retail calls it at four sites in `TSelectMenu::perform`). Identical codegen from a spelling with 15+ statements: which codegen-neutral constructs count.

## Structural pass V (2026-09-18): reference binders at zero frame cost and four real bugs

- **A TU-local `static inline` returning a *reference* to a member binds that member's address (`addi rD, rBase, off`) at the call site at zero frame cost**: closed `TApplication::initialize` on `TTimeRec::unk81C |= 1`. An existing reference-returning accessor does the same: `TSpineEnemy::getUnk104()` gives retail's `addi r4, r3, 0x104` where the raw `unk104.getPoint()` spells `addi r5, r3, 0x108` (`TTobiPuku::isReachedToGoalXZ`, `TTamaNoko::isReachedToGoal`, +2.4 each, +0x10 frame). - A named local for a nested call's result gives retail's `addi rD, r3, 0` + `mr` pair that a directly nested call does not (`TApplication::initialize_bootAfter`, both sites, to 100).
- A signed `cmpwi` on a `u32` parameter plus a `beq case; b default` pair is a one-arm `switch`, not `?:` (`TLimitKoopaFlame::receiveMessage` 75.7 -> 100).

US drops the shine-appear BGM restart in `MSound::mainLoop` (its only writer is already `#if !GMSE01`; 83.8 -> 100). `MSound`'s real size is 0xd8, not 0x310.
- not: `getUnk104()` inside `Enemy.hpp::isReachedToGoal` (whole-tree inert, fishoid's weak copy 100 -> 97); `TSpineEnemy::getSaveParam` through `TEnemyManager::getSaveParam()` (one level too many, limitkoopa -2.7, seven functions lost); reordering `TBigWindmill::load`'s three `TVec3` locals to retail's slot order (-0.45); `mVelocity.z*n.z + (x+y)` in `TTobiPuku::hitWall` (-0.45).
- Header items (round 32): `JAIBasic.hpp` `unk94` is `u32` (retail `lwz`/`stw`; `MSound::startMarioVoice` then wants `unk94 = param_1;` after `startSoundActorInner` and `unk94 != MSD_SE_MV13_ACTION_SMALL_01 &&` before two random substitutions, 7 instructions); `JUTNameTab.hpp` needs `u16 getNameNum() const { return mNameNum; }` (`TLimitKoopa::init` reads `8(r3)`); `MapData.hpp` `isWaterSurface()` groups 0x101-0x105 in one range test (`TPakkunSeed::rebirth` 98.45).

## Research batch 186 (2026-09-18): what MWCC counts as a statement, at zero codegen

Measured at depth 1 against a 14-filler base in a scratch TU with the game flags; every construct below leaves the callee's out-of-line body byte-identical, so the only effect is on the inline decision.

- **Counted (cost 1):** `a = a;`, `(void)0;`/`(void)a;`, `a += 0;`/`a *= 1;`, `if (0) {}`, `switch (x) {}` with or without cases, `while (0);`, `goto L; L: ;`, any statement after `return`, an unused `register int t = 1;`, an unused `{ int t = 1; }` block; `for (;;) { break; }` is 2.
  Dead stores (`int t = 0; t = 1;`) and comma expressions also count but change codegen.
- **Free:** `;`, `{}`, an uninitialised declaration, `{ int t; }`, `do {} while (0);`, a statement-level `asm {}`, a folded `const int k = 1;` (used or unused), `static const`, an `enum` or `typedef` in the body, a plain label.
- **The two plausible-source levers:** naming a subexpression (`int t = e; x = t;` costs 2 where `x = e;` costs 1, byte-identical) buys one statement; writing a callee's body out at the site buys its whole count (retail did it twice in `hamukuri`).
- Costs: `if (c) s;` 2, `if/else` 4, `if (c) return;` 2, ternary assignment 1, `for (i = 0; i < n; i++) s;` 5, and `if (a && b) s;` is 3, **the same as nested `if (a) { if (b) s; }`**: splitting a `&&` is not a lever.
- **The caller is inert**: a call inside an `if`, a `switch` case, a loop, nested blocks or a `while` + `switch` flips at 15 like a bare call, as do `this->callee()` and a qualified `C::callee()`. So a retail `bl` at depth 1 is only ever a count of 15 or more. `recoverFire` refusing to inline at depth 2 with 10 statements independently reconfirms the depth-2 budget of 9.
- Applied: `TFireHamuKuri::moveObject` 0 -> 100 by spelling `recoverFire` out inside `changeTevColor` (7 -> 17 statements; `changeTevColor` stays byte-exact at its 0x1cc map size, `recoverFire` stays as the listed helper); `TFireHamuKuri::isHitValid` 0 -> 100 by giving `requestSerialKill` its fifteenth statement (`if (obj == param_1) continue;` for the `&&`), which makes `THamuKuri::isHitValid` spell the same body out (98.4 -> 96.3: a callee-saved rotation plus a 16-byte frame gap that reads as two inline-temp slots; accepted for +2 exact).
- Not reachable this way: `TBossGesso::showMessage` (5 statements, `bl` at one of three sites: a depth case) and `TBossPakkun::showMessage` (7, per site); `SMS_getShineID` (`StageUtil.hpp`, about 10, needs +5: header round item); `TBossTelesa::forceAllItemKill` (12, `bl` at all three sites, needs exactly +3: three named locals such as hoisting `mSlotItems[i]`, a diff-driven closure item).

## Header round 32 (2026-09-18): range grouping is emergent, and a header helper's flip point is countable

- **Range grouping of `||` equality chains is emergent per expansion, not a source-order property.** MWCC compiles a chain of `x == c` tests over a contiguous constant run either as one range test (`subi`/`clrlwi`/`cmplwi`/`ble`) or as one individual test plus a range over the rest; retail's own `TBGCheckData::isWaterSurface` yields 28 grouped and 37 split expansions across 36 TUs from one header body (8 vs 1 inside `mameGesso.cpp`), ours 29/41 from the identical text, and the out-of-line weak copy in BeeHive is byte-exact. Reordering the terms only changes which constants the range covers, producing ranges retail never emits. A grouping mismatch at one site is a symptom of that function's other residue (`TPakkunSeed::rebirth`'s 0x20 frame gap), not of the predicate. Do not retry.
- **A header `static` helper's statement cost is measured like any plain function**: compile one `.cpp` with `if (0) {}` fillers and count `R_PPC_REL24` relocations against the symbol with `objdump -dr` (in a relocatable `.o`, `objdump -d` renders an unrelocated `bl` as a branch to its own address, so a name grep counts zero). `SMS_getShineID` (`StageUtil.hpp`) costs 8 and flips at 15; +7 lands retail's four `bl`s in `TSelectMenu::perform` (90.6 -> 93.3) but drops `TPauseMenu2::load` 99.96 -> 92.3, `TConsoleStr::load` 99.5 -> 93.8 and `TSelectMenu::initData` 91.7 -> 89.6, all of which reach it through `SMS_isGetShine` where retail has no `bl` at all. **Corrected by batch 190**: those three reconstructions are right (retail's expansions there are flat depth-1 and their `bl` counts already match at 8 statements, as do CardLoad's and Guide's), so the 15-statement body is refuted, not blocked; `TSelectMenu::perform`'s four missing `bl`s are an emergent per-expansion decision (see "Batch 190").
- `JAIBasic::unk94` (0x94) is a `u32` (retail `lwz`/`stw`); under GMSE01 the timer writes that looked like `unk94` are `MSound::mTimerParameter` at 0x9a, and the only reader is `MSound::startMarioVoice`, where it holds the last Mario voice id and gates two random substitutions (98.2 -> 99.8; the residue is a pure 0x30 frame gap). `JUTNameTab::getNameNum()` reads the joint bound at `8(r3)` (`TLimitKoopa::init` 96.3 -> 99.0; its named `MActorAnmBck* bck` local costs one `mr`: unit item).

## Batch 189 (2026-09-18): J3DModel's UNUSED bodies, three reloc bugs, and the named-local rule applied

- **J3DModel's 22 MISSING map symbols were all UNUSED bodies** (no weak copies), now defined in map order with 4 sizes exact and 18 size warnings; symbol order passes. The link test still changes the DOL: `J3DModel::entryModelData` is 99.8 (two register clusters, known-open), so the unit waits on that one function, not on symbols. `J3DSimpleModel` is reconstructed from its 0xc vtable (two nulls plus a dtor: it does not derive from `J3DModel`) and a four-instruction `lock()` at 0x10, which pins an **embedded** `J3DMatPacket` (a pointer needs a fifth instruction) and also lands `unlock` and the dtor at their map sizes.
- **Folding a multi-return `if`/`else` chain into ternaries is a caller-side inlining lever**: `TGesso::getSightDirection` (`Gesso.hpp`, absent from the map, so retail expands it everywhere) exceeded the budget at the `MsIsInSight` argument depth as nested returns and we emitted a weak copy; two ternaries took `walkBehavior` 81.6 -> 100 and `TNerveGessoStay::execute` 82.6 -> 99.9 at zero cost elsewhere. `check-relocs`' `O:<callee>` column names exactly this case.
- **A TU-local forwarder is a clean level for an implicit member-wise copy**: `static inline assignPathNode(TPathNode*, const TPathNode&)` in `enemy.cpp` keeps `TVec3::operator=` out of line as retail has it (`goToExclusiveNextGraphNode` 87.5 -> 99.8, plus `operator=` itself to 100) where the `PathNode.hpp` alternative was refuted for costing ~15 units. Add the level at the assignment in the `.cpp`, not in the copied class's header.
- **Research 186's named-local rule, applied on `TBossTelesa::forceAllItemKill`** (12 -> 15 statements, body instruction-exact, `forceHide` 49.6 -> 99.95, `TNerveBossTelesaDie` 90.0 -> 98.7, `PrepareSlot` 86.5 -> 98.4). The diff names which locals retail had: a callee-saved FPR holding a literal across a loop names that literal (`f32 zero = 0.0f`); an `addi` base reloaded through at every use names the **address** (`TLiveActor** slot = &mSlotItems[i]`), never the loaded pointer; a load feeding only a compare names the tested value (`THitActor* holder`). Conversely a local used more than once collapses reloads retail keeps (76.8), a local feeding a call argument costs an `mr`, and a loop bound in a local hoists (79.1).
- An address binding is **not** always +8 frame: `TVec3* pos = &(*slot)->mPosition;` at the `emit` site left the frame at 0x30 and cost one instruction; re-pass 162's +8 seems to need the binding consumed as a real `bl`'s receiver.
- Real bug: `TMuddyBoat::bind` passed `*gpMarioPos` where retail's argument loads ahead of the receiver (`SMS_GetMarioPos()`, the pass IV knob). Refuted as reloc rows: `TGessoManager::initSetEnemies` (frame-only, index artifact), `zigzagToCurPathNode` (`jmaSinTable`/`jmaSinShift` both present, shifted by the `JMASin` expansion).
- Header items (round 33): `JDRFlag.hpp` + `GameSequence.hpp`, force the by-value `TFlagT<u16>` parameter of `TGameSequence::set` to emit a real copy ctor (three MarDirectorDirect functions 95.1/87.2/63.2; evidence and four rejected spellings at `MarDirectorDirect.cpp:193`); `MapObjBase.hpp` `getObjCollisionHeightOffset` is a weak INLINED symbol retail `bl`s three times in `TMuddyBoat::bind` (MapObjMare's one MISSING symbol).

## Batch 190 (2026-09-18): `SMS_getShineID` is 8 statements, and an inlined callee's argument is not a level

- **`SMS_getShineID`'s cost is 8 and that is retail's cost.** Counting `R_PPC_REL24` relocations per function (retail asm vs our `.o`) across the five GC2D includers: `TPauseMenu2::load` 0/0, `TConsoleStr::load` 0/0 (byte-exact), `TSelectMenu::initData` 1/1, `TCardLoad::load`/`setupScoreScreen` 1/1 and 4/4, `TGuide::resetObjects` 4/4; only `TSelectMenu::perform` disagrees (retail 4, ours 0). Retail's `initData` both `bl`s it inside the `SMS_isGetShine` loop and inlines the byte-identical nested `strncpy(... SMS_getNormalStage(SMS_getShineID(...)) ...)` expression 0x520 later: the same TU, source and depth, one expands and one calls. `perform`'s four refusals are the emergent per-expansion class (`theNerve()`, Koopa's five blocks), not a cost or depth property; do not retry a 15-statement header.
- **An inlined one-liner's argument is not an inline level**: in a 50-line probe, `norm(gid(...))`, `(u16)norm(gid(...))` and `s16 id = gid(...)` all flip at the same cost (15); only being inside an inlined *body* refuses at cost 8. Sharpens the "Depth counts from the innermost expression" rule: the level is the enclosing inlined body, not the nesting of calls.
- `TSelectMenu::perform`'s sites had a real bug (sites 2 and 4 used the stale `shineID` local); respelled as retail's single nested statement, 90.6 -> 91.0.
- `TLimitKoopa::init` 99.0 -> 99.9 instruction-exact by dropping the named `MActorAnmBck*` local (`if (getMActor()->getAnmBck()) getMActor()->getAnmBck()->initSimpleMotionBlend(0x10);`, MWCC CSEs the second chain); removing the pointer local *grew* the frame by 8 (0x60 -> 0x68 against 0x70), so the residue is an 8-byte dead low region of an inlined callee.
- `MSound::startMarioVoice`'s 0x30 is 40 bytes of missing dead low region plus 8 of slack, and `startSoundActorSpecial`, `startBeeSe`, `talkModeOut` and `pauseOff` are all instruction-exact with the same kind of gap: one shared inlined callee in `MSound.hpp`/`MSoundSE.hpp` is missing a dead non-trivial local. Research item (whole-TU carrier).

## Structural pass VI (2026-09-18): `||` return chains, helper-vs-inline predicates, and five real bugs

- **Three `if (c) return;` in a row are one `if (a || b || c) return;`** when the last test is unfused (`bcc +8; b end`) and the earlier ones branch straight to the exit (`TElecNokonoko::behaveToWater`, `TPopo::behaveToWater` to 100; `TElecCarapace::perform` 98.4). The mirror for a `return true` body, all tests branching to one shared `li r3, 1`, closed `TGorogoro::isRolling` and `TIgaiga::isRolling`. Refuted: negating the guard and wrapping the tail in `if (...) {}` (MWCC folds it back).
- **A materialised bool means the predicate came from a helper; a direct branch means it was compared in place**, in both directions: `isBckAnm(N)` -> `mCurrentBckAnm == N` closed `TElecNokonoko::calcRootMatrix`; `mState == S` -> `isState(S)` took `TLeanMirror::touchPlayer` 90.7 -> 98.1.
- `x = !x` on a `u8` is `cntlzw`/`srwi`; `x = (x == 0)` adds a leading `neg` (`TElecCarapace::shoot`, +3.7).
- **Splitting a trailing divide off a float initialiser** (`f32 x = e; x /= k;`) lands the quotient straight in the local's callee-saved FPR and removes the `fmr` (`TBathtub::load` 99.65 -> 99.97); declaring the *copy* before the value it copies gives it f31 (`TTinKoopaFlame::emitFlameEffects`, 37 soft markers -> 17).
- A reference-returning accessor binds the member address (`getUnk104().getPoint()` gives `addi r4, this, 0x104`; both `isReachedToGoalXZ` 97.0 -> 99.68); the pointer form of the same idea did nothing for `TTinKoopa::updateTimers` or `SandCastleCallBack`'s camera vectors. An inline boundary on a test restores retail's second read of a member MWCC would CSE (`if (KumokunUnk1D0(this) > 0) unk1D0 -= 1;`, 98.0 -> 99.9; `-= 1` vs `= x - 1` inert).
- **A by-value `TVec3` parameter forwarded to another by-value parameter copies twice** where retail copies once (`TDirectionCalc::TDirectionCalc(TVec3)` -> `makeDirection(TVec3)`, six instructions per expansion); writing `makeDirection`'s body into the ctor closes `TKoopaJrSubmarine::makeRelativeAngle` (95.0 -> 99.8) but drops the ctor to 70.6, and `makeDirection` is by-value in the map: a trade, left open.
- **An `if` arm empty in our source but keeping its `cmpwi` in retail held a statement generating no code**: `a = a;` lands `TTinKoopa::reset` 99.9 and `TNerveTinKoopaBreak::execute` 100, not committed (a mechanism, not plausible source: find the real statement). An empty `case` is refuted as a filler (builds a range tree, -4.4), but a genuinely missing `case 1: break;` is the fix when retail's pivot has an extra arm (`TGoalWatermelon::control` 93.8 -> 96.9). A body retail expands at inline depth 4 where a plain call would not inline was written out at the site (`stopSound()` in `TOptionControl::adjustSound`, `checkInput` 95.2 -> 98.8).
- Real bugs: `gatekeeper` passed `0xE002E/0xE0047/0xE0000` to `startAppearBalloon` where retail passes `0x2B/0x4A/0` (three functions to 99.9+); `TShiningStone::putOnLight` called `setGlobalParticleScale(3,3,3)` where retail calls `setRate()` (95.2 -> 99.95); `TSandBase::withering` tested `==` for `<=`; `TYoshi::init` built `MAnmSound` for `MAnmSoundMario`, `getOff` lacked `mTongue->mState = STATE_IDLE;`, and the `thinkAnimation`/`thinkUpper` status constant is `0x80023C`, not `0x8023C`.
- Known-open, budget spent: `TIgaigaManager::createEnemyInstance` (retail `bl`s `TIgaiga(const char*)` at depth 1 though the ctor is size-exact at 0xa4 with 8 statements: the factory-ctor refusal class, now four); `TNerveBGK{Sleep,Dive,SleepDamage}::execute` (retail `add; subi 0x78; extsh` where MWCC folds `-120` into the random term; named intermediates and reorderings fold identically). cameragc: `getUnk1EC()` at either `C_MTXLookAt`/`MTXCopy` site is byte-identical; a TU-local `a && b` helper for `calcSlopeAngleX_` costs 2.5.
- Header items: `JGVec2.hpp`, `TVec2<f32>` has no copy ctor spelling giving the paired `lwz/lwz/stw/stw` (`TCameraMapTool(const TCameraMapTool&)` 91.7, the copy-ctor path, distinct from the `operator=`/`std::__copy` note); `Kumokun.hpp` `unk1D0` wants a `const` accessor (parked TU-local). `isWaterSurface()`'s pivot differs per site (`behaveToHitGround`/`TPopo::forceKill` want the split, `TNerveGorogoroDie` the merged form): confirms round 32's emergent grouping.

## Header round 33 (2026-09-18): copy elision is unconditional, a forwarding overload sinks a constructor body

- **Copy elision of a by-value class parameter is unconditional for an in-class copy constructor.** In scratch TUs with the real flags (`TGameSequence`/`TFlagT` shapes) MWCC never emits a call to an in-class copy ctor for the parameter of an inlined function taking a class by value: the argument's value category, a defaulted third parameter, an explicit member initialiser, a by-value enclosing-class parameter and inline depth 1-5 are all inert. Only a class-template member defined out of class without `inline` produces the call, refuted tree-wide for `TFlagT` (breaks JDRDStage/JDREfbCtrl/JDRViewConnecter). "Retail calls a copy constructor" is not a depth lever; MarDirectorDirect's `__ct__TFlagT<Us>(const&)` stays MISSING.
- **Depth ladder on a defaulted-argument constructor** (`TGameSequence::set(u8, u8, TFlagT<u16>)` under nested `static inline` levels): depth <= 2 everything expands; 3 puts the one-statement `TFlagT::set` out of line; 4 puts the three-statement `TGameSequence::set` out of line with the converting ctor called as `__ct__TFlagT(u16)` with an explicit 0; 5 emits the same call under the no-argument mangling `__ct__TFlagT()`. So the mangling of a defaulted-argument constructor call at a site tells which side of depth 5 it sits on.
- **A one-line forwarding overload on the owning class sinks a constructor body one level tree-wide**: `set(u8, u8) { set(p1, p2, TFlagT<u16>()); }` with `TGameSequence() { set(0, 0); }` in `GameSequence.hpp` lifted `TApplication::TApplication` 36.4 -> 72.8, `decideNextStage` 65.0 -> 77.7, `updateGameMode` +0.8, `TMenuDirector::direct` +0.2, nothing down. Unlike a defaulted argument it deepens the callee's body, not just the argument expression. Two levels overshoot; `TFlagT<u16>(0)` in the forwarder is worse; an explicit `: unk2(0)` is byte-identical.
- **`TVec3::set(x, y, z)` against three component assignments is a depth lever**: its arguments sit one level below the caller and evaluate right to left, so the last component lands in a callee-saved FPR first (retail's `f31` for `z` in `TMuddyBoat::touchWall`, UNUSED size unchanged at 0xa8). With a TU-local `static inline` binder over `getObjCollisionHeightOffset()` those are the two levels that put the accessor at depth 5: the weak symbol 0 -> 100 (MapObjMare's MISSING symbol recovered), `TMuddyBoat::bind` 80.5 -> 87.1; `set()` alone is worse (78.4), the pair is required.

## Structural pass VII (2026-09-18): chained stores, division direction, C89 aggregate placement, three real bugs

- A chained assignment `a = b = c = v;` stores **right to left**; four column-wise chains plus the diagonal are how retail writes an inline `Mtx` identity (`TSwingBoard::TSwingBoard` 94.4 -> 99.9).
- `x / 32.0f` keeps the dividend in the multiply's first source register, `x * 0.03125f` puts the literal there: read it off which `lfs` issues first (`TMario::doSliding` 98.5 -> 99.9).
- A `u16` local feeding a `u16` parameter is `addi rD, rS, 0` per site and truncates once at the definition; an `int` local re-masks (`clrlwi`) at every use (`THangingBridge::drawRopeBetweenBoards`).
- **MWCC C89 emits an aggregate local's initialiser at its declaration**, so retail initialising a `GXColor` mid-function means the declaration sits in a nested block; moving it to the top only moves the store (`Hxs_Logo_ExtraDraw`, `Hxs_Logo_TexSetup` to 100, with `GX_CA_A0`/`GX_CA_A1` swapped back and a dead `color.a = fade_in;`). `HxWork::step` is `u32` (`Hx_MovieStartSyncEx` to 100); `hx_buffer` is 32-byte aligned.
- A `u8` flag tested with a signed `cmpwi` and no `extsb` was read as `int`: `s8` adds `extsb.`, `u8`/`!= 0`/`> 0` give `cmplwi`; a `(int)` cast or an `int`-returning accessor is the fix (`TSwingBoard::control` 99.6 -> 99.9 with a TU-local one; header item: `TWaterGun::isEmitWater()` returning `int`).
- Two identical tail statements in the arms of nested `if`s are one `else`: fold the outer test into `&&` (`TNerveHanaSamboAttack::execute` 94.3 -> 99.9). `checkLiveFlag2` (BOOL) against `checkLiveFlag` (bool): retail materialises 1/0 then `cmpwi` (`TSamboHead::attackToMario` 94.6 -> 99.8).
- **Two discarded calls whose bodies open with a null test** carry the whole 0x40 frame gap with them (`TBathtubKillerManager::load` 81 -> 100: `getActiveObjNum()` before the base `load` and after the `new`).
- **A raw `TVec3` build is a consumption split**: interleaved load/store pairs in ascending offset are three assignments, all loads first in descending offset are a 3-argument `set`/ctor; both directions this pass (`THanaSambo::createPollen` 96.1 -> 99.7 one way, `TCoverFruit::calcRootMatrix` 93.7 -> 98.8 the other, with a named `J3DModel*` landing the frame). FPRs go f31-down in forward declaration order, so retail's extra named `f32` locals show as the register ranking (`TSwingBoard::drawOneRope` 86.5 -> 100 with three more named locals in retail's order).
- Real bugs: `TMario::running` tested `mNormal.y <= 0.17364818f` where retail has `>=` (both brake sites); `doRunningAnimation` computed the min where `sp` is `max(mIntendedMag, mForwardVel)`.
- Refuted: a TU-local `static inline` returning `const f32&` and a plain `const f32&` local both fold back to the member displacement and cost +8 (`TSwingBoard::draw`); `u16` indices in `setMActorAndKeeper` (-8); a discarded `(int)getFrame()` restores retail's `lfs`/`fctiwz`/`stfd` but costs 2.2 (`TNerveHino2Landing::execute`). Data lead: `TSamboFlower::moveObject` reads its `.rodata` strings 0x10 high (a `jIndexTable` placement difference, data-order item).

## Header round 34 (2026-09-18): a static's section reveals its constness; implicit copy ctors split on array vs class members

- **A function-local static's `const` picks its section**: `static const T x[]` goes to `.rodata`, `static T x[]` to `.data`, both mangled `name$NNNN`. The map's section for a `$NNNN` static is direct evidence about the keyword, and getting it wrong shifts every later string literal in the TU's `.rodata` by the array's size, which reads exactly like a pool permutation. `jIndexTable$3048` in `hanasambo.cpp` sits in `.data` between `entry$2896` and `entry$3090`; dropping `const` took the unit's data 55.5 -> 100 and three functions to 100. Find the `$NNNN` symbol in the map's section layout before chasing literal order.
- **MWCC's implicit copy constructor block-copies an array member with paired `lwz/lwz/stw/stw` but recurses to scalars (`lfs/stfs`) through a class-type member with no user copy ctor** (a class member with a user copy ctor is copied by calling it, as `TVec3<f32>`'s `*(Vec*)this = *(Vec*)&other` shows). So a two-word float pair word-copied inside an implicit copy ctor is an `f32[2]`, not a `TVec2`: `TCameraMapTool::mPitchYaw` is `f32[2]` (`TCameraMapTool(const TCameraMapTool&)` 91.7 -> 100, MarNameRefGen). The implicit `operator=` word-copies the whole aggregate either way (`std::__copy<TCameraMapTool>` was already exact).
- **Do not give `JGeometry::TVec2` a copy constructor**: a word-copying one buys that ctor but makes the class non-trivial tree-wide (`TMapCollisionData::intersectLine` 71.4 -> 57.3 with the frame 0x510 -> 0x530, SelectShine2 -0.5, `drawRopeBetweenBoards` -1.4, and the map-exact UNUSED `LineInLineXZ` 0x1bc -> 0x20c). Retail spells a `TVec2` copy at a call site component-wise, `TVec2<f32> v(other.x, other.y)`.
- `TWaterGun::isEmitWater()` returns `int` (signed `cmpwi` readers: MapObjMonte, MapObjBianco `TLeafBoat::control` 99.3 -> 99.9, MapObjMare `moveByWater` 98.5 -> 99.2; the raw `u8` read stays where retail has `cmplwi`: MarioCheckCol, MarioMove, WaterGun). `TKumokun::getUnk1D0()` replaces the TU-local.

## Structural pass VIII (2026-09-18): the big units, switch tells, load sharing and three real bugs

- **A dead `b <default>` left in a switch means one more empty case collapsing to that target** (`TBossPakkun::rumblePad` exact with `case 2: break;`; the same two-dead-arm signature is open in `TNozzleBase::animation`). `if (p != 2) return;` was a `switch { case 2: break; default: return; }` there (+0.4); `case 3: return;` turns it into a range test (-0.4).
- **A second `return v;` after `if (c) return f(v);` emits a dead `b epilogue`; `if (c) v op= k; return v;` shares the exit** (`TKoopa::getFlameDirRate` exact).
- **A named local declared *after* the expression that first materialises the value shares the load; declared before, it loads early and costs an `fmr`** (`TMario::thinkYoshiHeadCollision` 94.9 -> 99.6 by moving `f32 z = headPos.z;` one line down). A member read handed straight to a call gives `addi rD, rS, 0`; through a named local it gives `mr` (`TNozzleTrigger::movement` drops the local; `TCardLoad`/`CardSave` keep retail's). An `if/else` assigning constants writes the register directly, a ternary materialises into `r0` then copies.
- **`MsClamp<T>(v, l, r)` keeps a provably dead `v < l` test alive because `l` is a parameter**; a literal `else if (alpha < 0)` on a `u8` folds away. Four bosstelesa sites (`MsClamp<u8>(a, 0, 254)`, `TNerveBossTelesaFreeze` 94.2 -> 98.5): a surviving impossible compare is a `MsClamp` tell.
- **`s16_member = int_expr` costs an `extsh`; a bare `sth` means the RHS was already 16-bit** (`mTargetAngle = (u16)rand()`, while a `lha` read elsewhere pins the member signed). `unk1B4` is `s16` (`TBossWanwan::TBossWanwan` exact); `oldSelect` is `s8` (`waitForSelect3` exact); `isInWater` is `int` (`cmpwi`, not `clrlwi.`).
- **`MTXCopy(src_call(), member)` evaluates the destination first**, so the member address must be bound into a callee-saved register before the source call (`MtxPtr t = mDetachedMtx;`, `TBossEelTooth::receiveMessage` byte-exact).
- **Two statements retail performs under one null check** cannot be reached by calling the guarded wrapper and repeating the load: spell the guard out at the site and bind the wrapper's argument first (`TBossWanwan::init` +0.6). Dropping a cached `TGraphWeb*` and respelling the `getTracer()->getGraph()` chain at both sites lifted `emRunAwayToNearestNode` 98.1 -> 99.7.
- Real bugs: `TMario::checkRideMovement`'s first guard tests `groundActor`, not `wall` (98.8 -> 99.9, exact); `TDangoHamuKuri::calcRootMatrix` scaled `mPosition` for `mScaling` and called `this->getTakingMtx()` for `mHolder->getTakingMtx()`.
- **Refuted, do not retry**: `TNerveBase<T>::TNerveBase()` out of class (`Nerve.hpp`), although retail `bl`s it at every `theNerve()` static and the map has it weak 0x10 in Bird: fuzzy 97.68 -> 97.19, matched_code -3.15, DOL broken; the `bl` is per call site, exactly like `TVec3::set<f>`. `SMSGetPollution()` as the `stamp` receiver (byte-identical); a merged 3/4 range in a TU-local `isThing()` (net zero); `isHeadPulled` flattened to three returns (byte-identical, 0xa0 vs 0x70); `TVec3` ctor/`set` for `checkGraffito`'s ELECTRIC arm (+16 frame).
- Header items (round 35): `StageUtil.hpp` `SMS_isGetShine`'s `s32 shineId` is `s16` (measured: `setupScoreScreen` 98.6 -> 99.3, `TCardLoad::load` +0.1, zero regressions, DOL intact); `Graph.hpp` `TGraphTracer::popCurr()` binds `mPrevIdx` as a reference (`addi r4, r3, 8` before the `mCurrIdx` load); `Enemy.hpp` `getMaxHitPoints()` returning `int` restores bosstelesa's `clrlwi`; `JGMatrix34.hpp` `SMatrix34C<f32>`'s default ctor is a real weak 4-byte symbol in BeeHive that `TBWPicket`'s inlined ctor `bl`s (needs the out-of-class primary-template form; untested, tree-wide risk).

## Structural pass IX and header round 35 (2026-09-18)

- **A folded `A - x + B` that retail emits as `subfic A; addi B` means the subtraction was its own inline**: no operand order, paren form or statement split reproduces it, only a helper (`TGCConsole2::startDisappearTimer`/`startDownLeftBot` to 100 with a TU-local `GCConsole2HideOffsetY(const TExPane*)`; `startDisappearTank` 98.8 -> 99.7).
- **A member receiver must be a named local when retail loads it before the argument arithmetic**: a bare `unk124->mGlobalTranslation.set(...)` loads the pointer last and interleaves the `stfs`; the named local batches both `fmadds` (`processDownCoin` 93.9 -> 99.9, `processAppearCoin` 95.8 -> 99.9). A helper taking the receiver as a parameter is not the same (98.9): the level costs a row.
- **`case N: break;` on an empty first arm folds into the default and leaves the pivot tree; `case N: return;` keeps it** (`TRedCoinSwitch::control` 92.9 -> 95.0).
- The `if (a || b) return;` fusion scales to a whole prologue of early returns: read the groups off which tests are fused (`beq exit`) and which are unfused (`beq next; b exit` = last operand of its group) (`TMap::update` 96.9 -> 99.9 with two chains; `endCameraDemo` 99.1 -> 99.9).
- **A lone `addi rD, rBase, off` between two member loads in a tiny accessor is a reference-typed local**, not a missing level: `int& prev = mPrevIdx; int result = prev; int curr = mCurrIdx; ... prev = curr;` reproduces the `addi` and turns the write-back into `stw r0, 0(r4)` at zero frame cost (`TGraphTracer::popCurr`, `TNerveFireWanwanTurn::execute` 99.1 -> 99.7).
- **Narrow a local to the callee's declared return type before hunting register residues**: `SMS_isGetShine` held an `s16` result in an `s32`, a redundant `mr`/sign-extension pair at four inline sites (`setupScoreScreen` 98.6 -> 99.3, `TGuide::resetObjects` 94.9 -> 97.0, `initData`, `TCardLoad::load`). After any change to a helper's return type, re-check every local that stores it.
- Real bugs: `TGCConsole2::setTimer` left `timerValue` uninitialised when `param_1 != -1` and stored `param_1` for `timerValue`; `processDownCoin`'s blend-pane gate was inverted; `startInsertLife` lacked `unk1C4->getPane()->setAlpha(255)`.
- **`SMatrix34C<f32>`'s weak default ctor is a permanent residue class, do not retry**: the map's weak 4-byte `__ct__Q29JGeometry13SMatrix34C<f>Fv` (BeeHive) is `bl`-ed at ~20 sites while our in-class `{}` expands; the out-of-class forms available to an explicit specialisation (plain and `template <>`) emit a **global** symbol and break the link (batch 104's rows 4-5 confirmed on a second class), and making `SMatrix34C` the primary template costs 35 exact functions, -8920 matched bytes and the DOL (`TPolarCamera::perform` 99.7 -> 41.0). Same shape as `TNerveBase<T>::TNerveBase()`.
- `getMaxHitPoints()` returning `int` gains bosstelesa (`Freeze` 98.5 -> 99.7, `HideWait` +1.1, `PrepareSlot` +0.3) but costs `TFireWanwan::receiveMessage` 100 -> 96.2 (frame 0x40 -> 0x48, the removable 8-byte TU-local lever near `u8 maxHp = getMaxHitPoints();` in fireWanwan.cpp), `TFireWanwanTailHit::perform` -2.3, `PakkunRootCallback` -0.7, `TBossManta::init` -0.1: header round 36 item with the fireWanwan lever removal.
- Refuted: an `int&` parameter for `TLimitKoopaJr::updateTimers`' helper folds like `int*` (retail's load keeps the direct offset, only the store uses the bound address); a TU-local reference-returning inline for `TManhole::mInitialPosition.y`; a binder around `J3DModel::getBaseTRMtx`; `MsClamp(1.5f*maxDist + 300.0f, ...)` as one argument (moves the `fmr`); naming the height local against MWCC folding `+1` into the nearest subexpression. Header leads: `JDRLighting.hpp` `TAmbColor::getColor()` by value (`TLightCommon::loadAfter`'s 4-byte round-trip temp, 23 TUs read it); `TEffectColumWater::generate` needs the destination-copy side of the closed `operator*` split.

## Structural pass X (2026-09-18): call temporaries, the reference accessor's limits, and two real bugs

- **Three `TVec3` values built only to be passed as `const TVec3&` arguments are temporaries in the call, not locals**: right-to-left evaluation gives ascending low-region slots and reverses the store order; block locals get both wrong whichever order is chosen (`TBigWindmill::load` 90.6 -> 100).
- **The reverse of the named-local rule**: a pointer local that only feeds a call plus a couple of reads costs an `mr`/`addi`; retail re-calls the accessor and lets CSE pick the argument register (`TLampSeesawMain::loadAfter` 91.9 -> 99.8 and `setGroundCollision` 96.2 -> 99.8 by *deleting* the local).
- The embedded-`TPathNode` reference accessor (`getUnk104()`/`getUnkF4()`, `addi rD, rBase, 0x104` then +4) fixed four functions across two units (three TabePuku nerves, `TTelesa::isReachedToGoal`, `TTamaNoko::walkBehavior`) but regressed enemy.cpp, amiNoko.cpp and AnimalBase.cpp: measure per site.
- A member object's reference bound into a local (`TVec3& ownerPos = mOwner->mPosition;`) produces `lfsu` on the first component; `getPosition()` is not the same lever and is worse (`TTPHitActor::updateTerrainCollsion` 86.7 -> 93.0 with the reference, 84.4 with the accessor). Two callee-saved FPRs holding two of three components mean retail named exactly those two `f32` locals in the diff's order (`setPosAndInitAfterSinkBottom` +4.6). A `u8` loop counter against a `u16` getter gives `clrlwi 24` + signed `cmpw`, a `u16` counter `clrlwi 16` + `cmplw` (`TTelesa::init`).
- Retail materialises each of four `isState` results *and* the whole `||` chain once more: a predicate with a two-`return` body (`TAmiKing::moveObject` 97.1 -> 99.4, parked TU-local; belongs on `TMapObjBase`). An identity-then-overwrite `Mtx` binds its address once (`MtxPtr p = m;`, +8 frame, `TBiancoMiniWindmill::calc`).
- Real bugs: `TNerveTelesaImitate` had its sight test inverted (the imitating Boo revealed itself when Mario was *not* in sight; `beq <return false>` after `isInSight`, accepted -0.03); `behaveToBeTaken_`'s parasol balloon message is `0x52`, not `0xE004F` (97.2 -> 100).
- Header items: `JGVec3.hpp` `operator-` must reach `TVec3::sub` one level sooner than `fst -= snd` (naming `TVec3 span = flatEnd - flatStart; span.length()` takes `TMapWire::setFootPointsAtHanged` 92.4 -> 99.6 opcode-exact but pushes `sub` out of line in `getPosInWire` 85.9 -> 40.4; TODO in MapWire.cpp: the same a = b - c class); `MapObjBase.hpp` `waterHitPlane` returns `bool` not `BOOL` (`clrlwi.`, `TBiancoWatermillVertical::touchWater`); `TakeActor.hpp` `isHolding()` returns `BOOL` (`li 1/li 0/cmpwi`); `J3DModel::getBaseTRMtx()` sits one inline level deeper than ours (`addi rD, rS, 0; addi rD, rD, 0x20`; `TTabePuku::calcRootMatrix`, `TAnimalBird::calcRootMatrix`).

## Header round 37 (2026-09-18): the setter at the call site, and two return-type items refuted

- **`addi rD, rS, 0` followed by `addi rD, rD, 0x20` on a model pointer is a setter inlined at the call site, not a level inside the getter**: `MTXCopy(src, model->getBaseTRMtx())` folds the `+0x20` into one `addi`; `getModel()->setBaseTRMtx(src)` expands `MTXCopy(m, unk20)` one level down, forcing `this` out of r3 into r4 before the first argument and emitting retail's pair (`TAnimalBird::calcRootMatrix` 97.1 -> 100, `TTabePuku::calcRootMatrix` 99.0 -> 99.9). A `Mtx&` accessor plus forwarder and a `static` overload called as `getBaseTRMtx(this)` are byte-inert: a level over a pure `return member` decays. When a wrapper that would produce the shape already exists in the header, re-spell the call site before adding a level to the accessor.
- **One inlined `bool` accessor can be tested two ways, and only the `clrlwi.` site is the type tell**: `TTakeActor::isHolding()` is tested with `clrlwi. r0, r0, 24` in `TMario::checkCollision` and with `cmpwi r0, 0` in `TTPHitActor::updateTerrainCollsion` over the same `lwz 0x6c`/`li 1`/`li 0` prologue; `BOOL` breaks `checkCollision` (MarioCheckCol matched_code 92.4 -> 20.6) for +0.6 on TabePuku, and a `BOOL` local at the site keeps `clrlwi.` too. The `cmpwi` site is unexplained and is not a return-type tell (`TMario::checkStatusType` is likewise `bool`).
- **`TAmbColor::getColor()` stays `const TColor&`**: by value binds `TLightCommon::loadAfter`'s 4-byte round trip (+0.09) but costs a `stw/lwz/stw` copy plus 8 bytes of frame in every consumer that initialises a local from it (both `getAmbColor` overloads off 100); the first-read temp must come from a level on the read.
- `TMapObjBase::waterHitPlane` returns `bool` (`TBiancoWatermillVertical::touchWater` exact).

## Closure 228 (2026-09-19): five-function units, one real bug

- **A value retail loads directly into a callee-saved register** (`lwz r31, off(this)` with the compare reading r31) cannot be reproduced by any named local: a named local always materialises through a volatile plus an `addi` in the branch-taken block (`TMapStaticObj::perform`, function/block scope, init/uninit all inert; second confirmation).
- Naming a searched object is a signed per-site knob in both directions inside one function (`initUnique`: naming `TSky* sky` +0x10, naming the two `TDrawBufObj` results -8).
- Header item: `TMapCollisionBase::setMtx` (`MapCollisionEntry.hpp`) must **not** inline: the map has it weak with an unreferenced duplicate and all three retail sites (bosswanwan, MapStaticObject, MapObjBase) `bl` it, while we inline it everywhere and bosswanwan.o defines no `setMtx`; depth is not the cause (sites at depth 3 and 4 both `bl`). Closes `TMapStaticObj::init`'s last cluster.
- Structural tells left: `TGCLogoDir::direct_nlogo` (93.8) tests `mProgSelect->unkC.mValue` with signed `cmpwi` where we emit `cmplwi` (two sites) and computes `nextState = cond ? 1 : 3` into r0 then `mr r30, r0` after loading `gpMSound`; `TCoasterEnemy::moveCoaster` (82.7) reads the quaternion components 0x1a0, 0x1a8, 0x19c, 0x1a4 where we read 0x19c, 0x1a4, 0x1a0, 0x1a8 (the component pairing in the quat-to-direction block is wrong) and the frame is 0x58 too large. ObjHitCheck's five are the JGadget pool layout; MapObjRailBlock's are `indexToPoint` result temps 4-12 off at exact frames; coasterkiller `Manager::loadAfter` (+0x18, 13 instructions) is the batch's best remaining closure candidate.

## Header round 39 (2026-09-19): setMtx joins the weak-plus-bl refusal class; narrowing is a placement lever

- **`TMapCollisionBase::setMtx` is permanent residue, do not retry**: weak in three TUs with unreferenced duplicates and `bl`-ed at all three sites while our in-class body expands everywhere. Retail's `TMapObjBase::makeObjAppeared` settles the mechanism: its first arm expands the copy (`addi r4, r30, 0x20; bl PSMTXCopy`) and its second arm calls `setMtx`, same TU, function and depth, so the expanding arm is spelled as a direct matrix copy and `setMtx` is never inlined at all; body cost is one statement (the weak copy is 0x2c and compiles byte-exact). Out-of-class with `inline` is byte-identical to in-class (zero deltas); out-of-class without `inline` emits a **global** and breaks the DOL through the linked `Map/MapCollisionEntry.cpp`. Batch 104's rows 4-5 confirmed on a third class: for a non-template class there is no source shape (like `SMatrix34C<f32>`'s ctor). Side facts: `TMapStaticObj::init`'s frame is exact and its `Mtx` sits at 0xb0 vs our 0xb4 (a 4-byte named local above the matrix); `makeObjAppeared` is +0x20 short of retail.
- **A narrowing conversion is a placement lever, and the constant in a mixed-type ternary is where it goes**: `getMaxHitPoints()` keeps its `u8` return but its false arm is a plain `1`, so the ternary is an `int` narrowed once after the merge, which is retail's `clrlwi` (`TNerveBossTelesaFreeze::execute` 98.5 -> 99.7, `HideWait` +1.1, `PrepareSlot` +0.3, nothing off 100). Widening the return type re-narrows at every caller (effectEnemy unlinks). The narrowing belongs to whichever expression retail widened.
- **A reference-returning accessor on a params class is not the free binder**: `const TParamRT<u8>& getHitPointMax()` costs +0x10 at every expansion, priced by the bound object (as closure 219's `TVector` binder). `JGadget::TVector<T>::insert`'s 0x20 (DrawSyncManager) is pinned: retail's `InsertRaw` is UNUSED 0x38c and our object reproduces that size, so the MSL expansions sit at depth 2 as ours do and the surcharge cannot be paid by re-depthing.

## Closure 232 (2026-09-19): an UNUSED helper sized like a missing cluster is a missing call site

- **A big `<` cluster in a unit that also owns an UNUSED helper whose map size matches it is a missing call site, not a paste**: `startFadeIn__13TEndingStringFv` is UNUSED 0xe0 because retail inlines it at two sites in `TMovieDirector::direct` and dead-strips the body; our reconstruction had neither site (78.1 -> 99.3, all 550 instructions exact; residue 188 bytes of dead low region and a one-step rotation). ## Sub-30% sweep 243 (2026-09-19): array members, empty arms and the pivot, per-site refusals

- **The countable inline-refusal gap must be validated with `changes_all`, not one caller**: 7 filler statements in `TKoopa::changeAnm` take `TKoopaFlame::attack_` 18 -> 99.8 but turn `changeAnm` into a call everywhere else (11 functions, the unit 85 -> 76): a per-site refusal on a byte-exact 8-statement body (the emergent class). New measured gaps: `TLimitKoopa::startHipDrop` needs exactly 5 (`TNerveLimitKoopaHipDropStart::execute` 0 -> 99.9), `TLeanMirror::controlGoTarget` needs 3 **and** is instruction-exact with a 24-byte frame shortfall, so the three missing statements declare about 24 bytes of stack objects: a sharper search key than either signal.

## Sub-30% sweep 245 (2026-09-19): the factory-constructor refusal class is solved

- **A defaulted constructor argument at a `new` site is the one inline level that makes MWCC call an otherwise-expanded constructor**: `new T;` instead of `new T("name")` flipped `TIgaigaManager::createEnemyInstance` 15 -> 100, `TPakkunManager::createEnemyInstance` 26 -> 100, `TTelesaSlot::TTelesaSlot` 0 -> 99.8 (16-byte dead low region left), and `TMarNameRefGen::getNameRef_MapObj` to 100 (MarNameRefGen_MapObj 2.7 -> 99.0 matched code, one function from a link). Research 104 and 146 probed in-class bodies, base initialisers, vtables, virtual dtors, `__construct_array` and defaulted arguments always with the argument written out at the site, so the level was never there. The three "factory constructors retail calls" (`TTelesaSlot`, `TFence`, `TSunGlass`) leave the known-open list; `TSunGlass` was inert on one try and `TKoopaJrSubmarine`'s ctor stays exactly one statement short.
- **A bare `return;` costs 1 statement** (only `return <local>;` is free), so three early-return guards are 6 where the `&&` chain is 3: the early-return spelling is what makes 15 reachable (`TBossTelesa::forceHide` refused at depth 1, `TBossTelesaKillSmallEnemy::checkHit` 0 -> 98.6, `forceHide` itself exact with the two-argument `startSoundActor(id, pos)` at both sound sites, +8 each: the +8 rung does saturate over two exclusive branches when the function needs +0x10). The honest 15th was `BOOL playing = getMActor()->checkCurBckFromIndex(4);` (dies into the branch); naming the nerve hoists the spine load. **Explicit mem-initialisers do not count toward the inline budget.**
- The remaining sub-30% tail (39 functions, 1.7 KB) is almost entirely missing weak out-of-line copies (`TVec3::set<f>` x8, `MsClamp<f>` x3, `JUTRect`/`JSUInputStream` ctors, `SMatrix33C::at`): per-site inline refusals, a caller-frame problem.

## Constructor sweep 250 (2026-09-19): the matrix member's declared type is a depth lever; the theNerve level

- **The `new T("name")` lever has a member-type form**: where `check-relocs --mode calls` shows retail calling `SMatrix34C<f>::SMatrix34C()` at a `new TXxx(...)` site while we expand it, the missing levels are in the declared type of the constructed object's matrix member: `TMtx34f` puts `SMatrix34C()` at depth 3 (inlines), `TPosition3f` at depth 5 (called). `TBGTakeHit::unk80` and `TBWPicket::mTakingMtx` are `TPosition3f` (`TBGTentacle` ctor 97.9 -> 99.2, `TBossWanwan::init` 98.4 -> 98.9); `new TGorogoro;` moved the emitted call up to `TMatrix34<SMatrix34C<f>>::TMatrix34()` (`TGorogoroManager::createEnemyInstance` exact). **This family is invisible to `changes_all`** (objdiff scores a wrong-target reloc 100%): `check-relocs` is the only detector. A depth-0 local cannot be raised this way (`TLeanMirror::controlShake` inert).
- Real bug: `limitkoopa.cpp` assigned `calcTurnDirection`'s `f32` to `koopa->mBodyDirection` (a `TDirectionCalc`), invoking the conversion ctor; retail stores the float into `mBodyDirection.mDirection` (`TNerveLimitKoopaWait::execute` 74.8 -> 78.9, frame gap 0x30 -> 8).
- **Lead worth a dedicated batch**: `T:__ct__24TNerveBase<10TLiveActor>Fv` is missing at about 20 sites (BathtubKiller x6, KoopaJr, Koopa, bosstelesa x2, bosspakkun x2, Bird, Kazekun, chuuhana, hanasambo, cannon, hamukuri, elecNokonoko, limitkoopa, wireTrap): retail emits `li r3, instance$; bl TNerveBase<TLiveActor>::TNerveBase()` inside the inlined `theNerve()` guard, we store the vtable word inline; `DEFINE_NERVE`'s `static Name instance;` puts `TNerveBase()` at depth 3-4 for us, retail needs 5, and `TLimitKoopaHead::receiveMessage` shows the whole site (with `pushNerve`) one to two levels lower in retail. Structural pass VIII refuted making `TNerveBase()` out of class (-3.15 matched, DOL broken): the level is at the sites. `JUTRect(int, int, int, int)` is a weak 0x30 emitted from Option.cpp in retail that we never emit (an INLINED-class lead).

## Research batch 251 (2026-09-19): the theNerve() base constructor is an inline-depth gauge

- Measured on scratch TUs with the game flags: with `theNerve()` inlined at level 1 the `TNerveBase<T>::TNerveBase()` expansion (`lis/addi/stw` of the base vtable) survives at every guard in the function; **at level 2 the first guard expands and every later one emits `li r3, instance$; bl TNerveBase<TLiveActor>::TNerveBase()`**; at level 3 `theNerve()` itself goes out of line. Depth, not budget: 64 inlined statements ahead of the guard change nothing.
- So a `T:__ct__24TNerveBase<10TLiveActor>Fv` row from `check-relocs --mode calls` means the site is one level too shallow, and the level is **not on the push side** (a `pushNerve` forwarder, a second forwarder, or binding `&X::theNerve()` to a local are byte-inert: an argument is evaluated at the caller's level). **The missing level is a named helper the map already lists as UNUSED whose body we pasted at the site**: `TKazekun::isDamage()` is `{ return isHitWater(); }` (0xf0 against `isHitWater`'s 0xf4; `attackToMario` 93.8 -> 100), `THanaSamboHead::receiveMessage` calls `THanaSambo::waterDamage()` (UNUSED 0x140; 96.2 -> 100), `TBossTelesaBody::receiveMessage` calls `TBossTelesa::checkMessage` (UNUSED 0x24c) and the tongue `tongueHitWater()` (UNUSED 0x11c), both 95 -> 100. `Nerve.hpp` untouched: an explicit `Name() {}` in `DECLARE_NERVE` is not a level; structural pass VIII's out-of-class refutation stands.
- Open with no named helper in the map: Bird `receiveMessage` (both sites, one nerve), BathtubKiller x6, koopajr, Koopa, limitkoopa, wireTrap, elecNokonoko, chuuhana, cannon, hamukuri, bosspakkun x2 (frame/regalloc-dominated residues).

## MapMirror reconstruction 249 (2026-09-19): depth probes inside one caller, and scaleAdd's operand order

- `TMirrorModelManager::perform` 32.6 -> 82.5 (block 1 instruction-exact): `if (cue & CUE_MOVE)` gates block 1, both the flag check and the plane copy read `gpMarioGroundPlane[0]` through one `setUnk84AndUnk90`, `unk1C[unk18]->calcView()` replaces the hand-expanded `viewCalc()`, and `entry()` follows `setPlane()` under `CUE_ENTRY`. `makeMirrorViewMtx` (0x120), `calcEffectMtx` (0x70) and `calcView` (0x24) are now at their map sizes; `entry` 0x1f8 vs 0x200; the weak `TVec3::scaleAdd` is emitted (missing -> 97.7) because `makeMirrorViewMtx` reaches it at depth 4.
- **A function inlined twice in one caller at depths 2 and 3 is a free depth probe**: `scaleAdd` (3 statements) expands at 3 and is called at 4; `set<f32>` (3) and `dot` (1) are called at 4; `TVec3()` (0) inlines at 4 and is called at 5; `dot` called while `scaleAdd` expands in the same statement is the tell that the scale factor is computed one level down. A two-object declaration (`TVec3 a, b;`) and a two-element array both count **2** statements; a forwarder does not remove its statement from the caller; `inline` on a member's declaration flips the symbol weak but buys no depth. A 1-statement in-class member (`calcDistance`) does not inline at depth 4. The only plane split keeping 0x120 is `MapMirrorPlane{mNormal, mD}` + `MapMirrorVecs{mTarget, mUp}`.
- **Header item**: `JGVec3.hpp`'s `scaleAdd(scale, b, c)` is spelled `x = b.x + c.x * scale` but the game's only out-of-line copy (this TU) is `lfs f2, 0(r4); lfs f0, 0(r5); fmadds f0, f2, f1, f0`, i.e. `x = b.x * scale + c.x`; flipping the body **and** the b/c arguments at all ~15 sites (cameralib x10, sunmodel, JPAParticle, CameraJetCoaster, CameraBGCheck, graph, fireWanwan) cancels exactly elsewhere and fixes 6 of 13 instructions in `scaleAdd` plus 6 in `perform`.

## Header round 41 (2026-09-19): scaleAdd's operand order

- `TVec3<f32>::scaleAdd(scale, b, c)` is `x = b.x * scale + c.x` (the first reference parameter is the scaled one), from the game's only out-of-line copy (MapMirror: `lfs f2, 0(r4); lfs f0, 0(r5); fmadds f0, f2, f1, f0`). Flipping the body and swapping b/c at all 32 sites cancels exactly (only MapMirror moves; `scaleAdd` 97.7 -> 100). `TVec3::scale(f32)`, `scale(f32, const TVec3&)` and `TVec4::scale` are already right against their boid/BeeHive copies. Method note: grep a header helper's call sites without `head`; a truncated listing hid two MapObjCorona sites and the header-only build blamed three unrelated `TBathtub` frames.

## Paste sweep 252 (2026-09-19): the paste-vs-call swap pays only across an inline boundary

- **The paste-vs-call swap is byte-neutral unless the helper's own body straddles an inline boundary**: `TMario::calcBaseMtx` calling its three UNUSED helpers (0x120/0xc8/0xa4) instead of spelling them out is identical to the byte (frame still 0x2d0 vs 0x2f0). Closure 248's rule is narrower than it reads: the extra level pays only when something *inside* the helper (a weak copy, a `MsWrap`, a `TNerveBase()` guard) sits at the limit; a helper made of direct calls and stores is invisible to the swap. Rank candidates by what the helper contains, not its size.
- **An interleaved statement at the paste site refutes the call outright** (`TGuide::perform`: `startSoundSystemSE` sits between `disappearGuidePane`'s `bounds` fetch and its `setCenteredSize`, -5.4 when hoisted); **a paste that differs from the helper body by one accessor is not that helper** (bosswanwan's GraphWander uses `boss->getPosition()` where `rollNextGraphNode` uses `mPosition`, -3.1).
- Detector that works: for each unlinked unit, every map UNUSED symbol the `.cpp` defines but never calls, scored by how many of its statements appear verbatim elsewhere in the file (38 hits, `paste.txt` in the batch's scratch). `check-relocs --mode calls` own-class `T:` rows are the per-site inline-refusal class (`getWireDir`, `updateRumble`, `setSpicy`, `showMessage`, `getBodyMaxRotateZ`, `execMotionBlend_`), not pastes. Guide (8.2) is blocked on `ExPane.hpp`'s `setPaneAlpha`/`setPaneSize` weak copies (shared header).

With that level and 7-statement bodies `perform` reproduces `bl setPaneSize` and `bl setPaneOffset` from inside the inlined `setCenteredSize` while the explicit depth-2 `setPaneOffset` two statements later still expands: the pair needs 7-9 statements and `setPaneAlpha` 10-14, for which its 160-byte weak copy has no room (our in-class bodies are the weak copies verbatim). Rejected; `setPaneOffset` is not a puzzle (weak in GCConsole2 at depth 4, budget 2, already exact).
