## Closure 254 (2026-09-19): a four-float constructor, depth-4 room, and the deferred accessor load

- **A class-type local built from member reads that must survive a call is a four-parameter constructor, not a `this`-taking one**: `MapMirrorPlane(f32 x, f32 y, f32 z, f32 d)` reproduces retail's right-to-left argument evaluation into f28-f31 across `bl TVec3<float>::TVec3()` and the `mD` store after `bl set<f32>` (`TMirrorModelManager::perform` 82.4 -> 86.5, `TMirrorModel::entry` at 0x200 exactly). **`: mNormal()` is not free** (qualifies "explicit mem-initialisers cost 0"): it and `: mD(d)` both cost, and `: mD(d)` moves the store ahead of the `bl`. **The depth-4 allowance of 2 is spent one by an implicit member construction**: a ctor that must emit a member ctor call has one statement of room at depth 4.
- **Reading a member through an inlined `const` accessor defers its load past a directly read global member and flips which operand lands in the destination FPR**: `getUnk80() * gpCamera->mFovy` gives retail's `fmuls f1, f0, f1` (`drawSetting`, `TMirrorCamera::perform`, `calcEffectMtx` inlined: three sites at once).
- Bird: a TU-local wrapper of the `mSpine->reset(); mSpine->setNext(...)` block does not buy the level (96.9 -> 91.5); `checkChangeToItem` (0x124 vs 0x110) and `isChangeToItem` (0xac vs 0x98) are both exactly +0x14, so the missing level is below `isChanged()`, not around the `setNext` block. MapMirror frame ledger: `isUpperThanMirrorPlane` -8, both `init`s +0x18 (+0x14 low plus a 4-byte hole above the `TPosition3f`), `setPlane` -0x10, `initPlaneInfo` -0x48, `drawSetting` +0x10, `TMirrorCamera::perform` +0x40; the ctor's residue is `GXInitTexObj`'s argument evaluation direction. Leads flagged by `check-relocs --mode calls`: Bird `WalkOnGround` (missing `TVec4<f>::TVec4()`/`set<f>`), `doLanding` (missing `TUtil<f>::sqrt`).

## Guide/Option follow-up 256 (2026-09-19): clapAlpha is in no map; the ternary join temp; the argument-side accessor

- `TGuide::disappearGuidePane` is 0x344 exactly with `startSoundSystemSE(MSD_SE_SY_TALK_MODE_OUT, ...)` between its two `JUTRect` copies and the `height`/`width` locals, and `perform` calls it (`perform` 78.8 -> 77.8 accepted: the inline level retail has).
- **`clapAlpha` is in no map at all**: a fabricated helper, and our `bl clapAlpha__Fs` in `linkSelect` is a link blocker. Retail inlines everything at the Option sites (`setPaneAlpha` at depth 1, the clamp at depth 2, `J2DPane::setAlpha` a bare `stb 0xcc`). The honest form is its two statements inside `TExPane::setPaneAlpha` (`s16 a = initial_alpha < 0 ? s16(0) : initial_alpha; if (a > 255) a = 255; mPane->setAlpha(a);`).
- **A single `return cond ? a : b;` in an inlined body costs an 8-byte join temp where `if (a > 255) return 255; return a;` costs none**, and no call-site level cancels it (`TOptionSubtitleUnit::setState` 0x258 vs 0x250 under the ternary form).
- **An argument-side member accessor is a clean +8 rung** where the receiver-side binder saturates at +16 and receiver-side forwarders are +0: `u8 getInitialAlpha() const` on `TOptionRumbleUnit` and `TOptionSoundUnit` at their `setPaneAlpha(30, 150, mInitialAlpha)` sites. Measured recipe for header round 43 (reverted here): the folded `setPaneAlpha` + the two accessors gives all three `TOptionXxxUnit::setState` 100, `linkSelect` 93.7 -> 95.5, Guide +0.4; weakening Option's two fabricated binders (`OptionGetPane`, `OptionParentPane`) to direct-return forks then lands `TOptionSubtitleUnit::TOptionSubtitleUnit` at 100 (0x1a0) with `update` 8 short.
- `TGuide::checkPoint` (99.8): one missing inlined callee (28 B of low region) plus a misplaced 4-byte named local above the first `JUTRect`.

## Header round 43 (2026-09-19): the clapAlpha cut lands, and the binder is exactly +8 per site

- Follow-up 256's recipe held: `clapAlpha` deleted, its statements inside `TExPane::setPaneAlpha` as `s16 a = initial_alpha < 0 ? s16(0) : initial_alpha; if (a > 255) a = 255;`, plus `u8 getInitialAlpha() const` on `TOptionRumbleUnit`/`TOptionSoundUnit` at their `setPaneAlpha(30, 150, ...)` sites: all three `TOptionXxxUnit::setState` exact, `TGuide::linkSelect` 93.7 -> 95.5, `TGuide::perform` 77.8 -> 78.8, `bl clapAlpha__Fs` gone from Guide.o; `TCardLoad::titleDraw` 99.92 -> 99.77 (frame 8 short: CardLoad wanted the level the helper supplied).
- **A TU-local binder (`T* x = p->m; return x;`) is worth exactly +8 of low region per call site; a fork (`return p->m;`) and a raw member read are both +0**: all 27 arrangements of `TOptionSubtitleUnit::update`'s three `mParentPane` reads give frame = 0x1e8 + 8 x binder sites, the map's 0x1f0 needs exactly one, and the twelve one-binder arrangements are byte-identical, so which site carries it is unobservable. `OptionGetPane` forked puts `TOptionSubtitleUnit::TOptionSubtitleUnit` at 100 (0x1a0).
- Rejected: `if (a < 0) a = 0; if (a > 255) a = 255;` in `setPaneAlpha` pushes the whole function out of line (the weak copy appears at 92.1 and `TGuide::perform` jumps to 84.6 on retail's `bl`) but every `setState` falls off 100 and matched code drops 0.07: the out-of-line `setPaneAlpha` and the Option sites cannot both be had from one body.
